<multi_agent_role>You are `/root`, the primary agent in a team of agents collaborating to fulfill the user's goals.

At the start of your turn, you are the active agent.
You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents.
All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent without triggering a turn.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.
You can decide how much context you want to propagate to your sub-agents with the `fork_turns` parameter.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
They may be addressed as to=/root

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

<INSTRUCTIONS>
# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

# AGENTS.md

This file is the Codex-facing adapter for this repository. It summarizes the Claude-oriented project guidance from `CLAUDE.md` in a smaller format that is fast for Codex to scan. `CLAUDE.md` remains the fuller source of project knowledge.

## Fast Locator

- Vault root (the `~/ai-os` directory IS the Obsidian vault): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os`
- Code repo root (this project): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/projects/lsatu`
- Live vault knowledge: `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/wiki/` (hubs, voice, business), `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/students/`, `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/corpus/`
- Pre-2026-07-16 LSATU product knowledge (archived, read-only): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/`

If an older note says `~/Documents/Obsidian Vault/...` or `~/Documents/Claude/02_PROJECTS/LSATU`, treat it as stale — the vault was consolidated into `~/ai-os`. Use the absolute paths above.

## Project Map

- `src/App.tsx`: route registration and top-level app wiring.
- `src/pages/`: route-level screens, including the Foyer and admin/teacher surfaces.
- `src/components/`: shared UI, including `foyer/`, `inbox/`, and shadcn/ui primitives.
- `src/hooks/`: auth, permissions, and shared data hooks.
- `src/lib/`: drill, WAJ, gamification, and other product logic.
- `src/integrations/supabase/`: generated Supabase client and types. Do not hand-edit generated types.
- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.
- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.

## Stack And Commands

- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.
- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.
- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.
- Build: `npm run build`
- Lint: `npm run lint`
- Test: `npm run test`
- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`
- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.
- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).

## Working Rules

- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.
- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.
- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.
- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.
- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.
- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.
- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.
- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.

## Product Constraints

- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.
- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.
- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.
- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.
- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.

## Smart Drill Note

On `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.

## Verification (must not skip)

- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not "done" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.
- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed "fix" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.

## Workflow Expectations

- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.
- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.
- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.
- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.
- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.

## Session Protocol (second brain)

Product context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.

The vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student's record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.

- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.
- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong "Done" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.

`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.

## Codex Adapter Layout

- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.
- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.
- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.

This checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.

## Known Gaps

- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.
- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.
- `README.md` is still minimal.

## Agent skills

### Issue tracker

GitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.

### Triage labels

The five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.

### Domain docs

Multi-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.

Accuracy: "first attempt" = the earliest eligible row per \r\nstudent and question across ALL history, then windowed. Volume: deduplicated within each local day only. A question \r\nwith any undated eligible row is excluded from first-attempt accuracy and counted in a disclosed "n questions without \r\ndates left out". No timestamps are invented; no rows are rewritten. Tests: older original + recent retry; original \r\nfollowed by its BR row sharing a mode; an ordinary row with BR flags false; undated original + dated retry.\r\n  - **Accuracy trend tile ("First-attempt accuracy, last 8 weeks").** All 8 weekly buckets are kept in the data and \r\nthe accessible table. A week with >=10 eligible first attempts is a solid point; 1-9 is a hollow, unconnected marker \r\nlabelled "few questions"; 0 is a gap labelled "No first attempts" (volume describes practice activity); \r\nnon-overshooting monotone interpolation between adjacent points only; the current week is labelled "so far" and glows \r\nonly if it is a point. Fewer than 3 weeks with at least 10 eligible first attempts (hollow markers do not count) -> a \r\nlabelled simple result ("62% on 24 first attempts since Sept 1"). Each point shows correct/total on focus/hover and in \r\nan accessible table.\r\n  - **Volume tile ("LR questions, last 7 days").** Bars per local day = distinct eligible question ids that day (a \r\nrepeat on a different day counts again, disclosed). The headline = the sum of the bars. Current day labelled "today".\r\n  - **Proportions.** Accuracy tile 7/12, volume tile 5/12, equal heights, big light numerals for the real headline \r\nvalues (Homies hierarchy), direct labels, plot area at least 60% of tile height. Dark surfaces with a restrained warm \r\nlight behind the plot (Notton/WeatherWise depth); static under reduced motion.\r\n  - **Per-type list (section 5)** keeps its population, "latest attempt per question within each 30-day bucket", and \r\nis labelled so; it is styled as plain continuous bars, not Be.run segments, so it does not read as task completion. \r\nThe two populations are labelled distinctly and never compared as improvement.\r\n  \r\n  A4. **Main card is full width** (PR 1: the "pick up where you left off" hero + assigned work; text width capped \r\naround 68ch, height content-driven). PR 2: the full-width insight card has diagnosis/action on the left and bubbles on \r\nthe right, stacking under 900px container width.\r\n  \r\n  A5. **Assigned work has a permanent home.** From PR 2 it moves out of the insight card into its own "Assigned to \r\nyou" card (Coders-style compact rows: title, due date, real progress where the model has it) in the lower row, keeping \r\nopen, briefing interception, Mark done and report-card seen. Be.run-style progress with a clear endpoint is used only \r\nfor real completed/total counts (hero items, bootcamps).\r\n  \r\n  A6. **Last section (PR 2).** Needs the bounded sections adapter from section 2, not `sectionHistory.ts:107` (it \r\nzero-fills scores and can invent a completion date). Latest row with a known completion date; unknown scores or \r\ndenominators shown as unknown; its Review link opens that saved result, never a new section (verified in PR 2).\r\n  \r\n  A7. **One authoritative order.**\r\n  - PR 1, desktop: top panel (greeting, status, slim next-session line, days to test | finder, Continue, pills incl. \r\nBook) -> main card (full) -> From Joshua (only when a message exists, full width, compact) -> Next seven days.\r\n  - PR 2, desktop: top panel -> insight card (full) -> chart row (accuracy 7 | volume 5) -> per-type list (7) + Last \r\nsection (5) -> Assigned to you (7) + From Joshua (5) -> Next seven days.\r\n  - Phone (one column): top panel (containing the one Continue) -> diagnosis/action -> accuracy -> volume -> bubbles \r\n-> per-type list -> Last section -> Assigned to you -> From Joshua -> week.\r\n  - Charts must not be buried (Codex round 2). Claude keeps the insight first because Joshua chose diagnosis-to-action \r\nto lead (round 10), but bounds what sits above the charts: on phones the top panel is compact (finder, one Continue, \r\npills in two rows), and the insight shows diagnosis + action with the bubbles moved below the chart row. Browser \r\ncheck: at 390x844 the chart row starts within the second screen; at 1440x900 it starts within the first screen and a \r\nhalf. These are acceptance checks at normal text size, never fixed heights that clip at 200% text. Codex accepted \r\ninsight-first in round 3.\r\n  - Collapse: any absent tile\'s partner spans the full row; a row with nothing renders nothing; loading shows \r\nlayout-matched skeletons, errors show retry, neither shows as empty.\r\n  \r\n  A8. **Build order.** PR 1 revision on the uncommitted build: A1, A2, A4, A7 (PR 1 order); no new data beyond the \r\nexisting `test_date` read. PR 2 = section 2 RPCs + A3 + A5 + A6 + bubbles/list/evidence + `excludeAnswered`. Builder: \r\ngpt-6-luna high (Joshua, 2026-09-23). Staging excludes `supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts`, which the dev server \r\nregenerates.\r\n  \r\n  ## Approach\r\n  \r\n  ### 0. Preservation checklist (every PR)\r\n  Assigned work rows and actions; timed-section briefing interception; report-card seen tracking; message preview \r\ndismissal watermark; `HomeAccess` gates; Question finder never gated; coach account never offered assigned work; Next \r\nseven days strip markup, classes and CSS unchanged; today\'s full Classroom menu unchanged (v3\'s slim rail is NOT \r\nbuilt).\r\n  \r\n  ### 1. Layout\r\n  1. **Top panel** (full width, warm gradient): date line, greeting, status line, two numbers: LR questions this week \r\n(counted from `attempts` in a new `security invoker` RPC `home_week_counts(p_class_id, p_tz text)`: distinct questions \r\nanswered in the student\'s last 7 local days, excluding review and blind-review replays; label says LR; NOT \r\n`daily_stats`, which nothing writes, verified 2026-09-23; test: finishing a drill question raises the number) and days \r\nto test day (`profiles.test_date`; hidden when null). Right: Question finder bar; dark Continue card (earliest-due \r\neligible assigned work; hidden when none); Smart Drill (`kind: \'smart-drill\'`); Timed section (goes to the Testing \r\nCentre `/testing`, which already starts LR and RC sections with timing and accommodations; same `has_drill_access` \r\ngate; replaces #133\'s finder action); Inbox with the existing unread count to `/inbox`.\r\n  2. **Main row**: main insight (8/12, taupe) + Next session (4/12, dark). No booking: compact "Book a session" \r\n(existing CTA), never hidden or an empty shell.\r\n  3. **Lower row**: per-type list (7/12) + tiles (5/12): Last section; From Joshua (existing latest-message preview); \r\nWrong answers to revisit (journal items due this week).\r\n  4. **Next seven days**: unchanged.\r\n  Columns by container width (existing container query); one column under 900px container; phone order: top panel, \r\nContinue, main insight, Next session, list, tiles, week.\r\n  \r\n  ### 2. Data layer\r\n  **Adapter states.** Every adapter returns one of `loading | error | empty | insufficient | ready`. Only `empty` \r\n(confirmed zero rows) may produce "start here" copy; `error` shows "Could not load this, retry" with the last good \r\nvalue and its date if cached; `insufficient` shows the labelled single-result fallback. The engine never turns `error` \r\nor `loading` into an empty claim.\r\n  \r\n  **New RPCs** (additive migration), all `security invoker`, `set search_path = public`, matching \r\n`20260709235900_phase6_query_rpcs.sql:25`, so existing RLS on `attempts` / `section_history` decides access; `p_days` \r\nbounded 7..90; tests: anonymous denied, student A cannot read student B, admin can read any.\r\n  - `home_lr_type_accuracy(p_class_id, p_days int default 30)`: two buckets with one shared cutoff: `current` = (now - \r\np_days, now], `prior` = (now - 2*p_days, now - p_days]; each bucket picks the latest attempt per distinct question \r\ninside that bucket, excluding review / blind-review replays; returns per qtype and bucket: questions, correct, missed, \r\nlast_attempt_at.\r\n  - `home_lr_type_evidence(p_class_id, p_qtype, p_days, p_cutoff timestamptz)`: the qids behind the current bucket. \r\nThe aggregate returns its `cutoff` (the `now()` it used); the frozen insight passes that cutoff to the evidence call, \r\nand both use one shared SQL selection function with the same cutoff, so evidence opened later matches the numbers \r\nshown.\r\n  \r\n  **Sections adapter** over `section_history` (not the zero-filling `sectionHistory.ts:113` reader): denominator = \r\n`initial_total` when > 0, else `total_questions` when > 0, else unknown (row shown without a fraction, never `x/0`; \r\nsee `sectionHistorySave.ts:324`); blind-review figures only when `br_used` is true; unknown dates stay unknown \r\n(excluded from recency), label "Sections" unless `mode` proves timing; blind-review delta shown as evidence only.\r\n  \r\n  **Section diagnosis**: top saved finding from `analysis_json` via `sectionAnalysis.ts`.\r\n  \r\n  **RC**: not a lens in this build (its readers are unbounded, `rcProgress.ts`); deferred with a bounded aggregate to \r\nbuild two.\r\n  \r\n  ### 3. Insight engine (fixed rules, no AI text)\r\n  Pure `pickInsights(signals)` in `home/insights.ts`, fixture-tested. Lenses: LR types, Sections, Section diagnosis. \r\nFloors: types ≥8 distinct questions in `current`; sections ≥1 in 21 days. Score = recency + actionability, normalized \r\nper lens; fixed tie order; one card per source. The chosen insight is frozen for the visit; new data shows a quiet \r\n"New results, refresh" control and never reorders under the pointer.\r\n  \r\n  ### 4. Diagnosis-to-action (leads the main card)\r\n  - Registry `home/actionRegistry.ts`: 4-8 entries `{ findingRule, launch body or route, reason }`, drafted by Claude \r\nfrom existing bootcamps, guided drills, Read Recall Colour and timed sections; Joshua reviews once before merge.\r\n  - Card order: headline (what the evidence shows); the recommended action with its one-line reason; "Practice <type>" \r\n(`kind: \'filter\'` with that qtype); "Review these" (journal entries of that type, when any); "See the evidence". \r\nUnmapped finding: "See the evidence" replaces the recommendation.\r\n  - **New-questions-only**: "Practice <type>" prefers unseen questions. Requires an additive `excludeAnswered?: \r\nboolean` on the filter launch body, applied BEFORE sampling where the bank is filtered (`Drill.tsx:565`), with the \r\nbuilt queue persisted in the Run Record so resume replays it. The freshness policy joins the resume identity \r\n(`drillResumeSession.ts:93`) so a fresh-only run never resumes an unrestricted one; links without the flag keep their \r\nexisting identity. Test: unfinished unrestricted run, then fresh-only launch of the same filter starts a new run. \r\nShortfall: "Only N new <type> questions left" with "Include ones you have answered". This ships in the same PR as the \r\nbutton (PR 2), never after.\r\n  - Evidence drawer: exact addresses from the evidence RPC or section `questions_json`; counts, window, "updated \r\n<time>".\r\n  \r\n  ### 5. Honest visuals\r\n  - Bubbles: area proportional to missed (radius = k * sqrt(missed)), computed, never hardcoded; each labelled with \r\ntype, accuracy, "missed of attempted"; two-size legend. Bubbles are decorative and non-interactive; selection happens \r\nthrough the list rows (real buttons), and selecting a row highlights its bubble. Only types with missed > 0 get a \r\nbubble; zero-miss types appear in the list only. Fewer than 3 types with misses: labelled results instead of bubbles. \r\nEvery eligible type at zero misses: a result state ("No misses in [n] questions over 30 days") with Practice harder \r\nquestions.\r\n  - Labels never white on orange: bubble text is ink `#1f1b18` on the orange fill, or white only on the dark bubble; \r\nevery text/fill pair verified ≥4.5:1 (≥3:1 at 24px+) in light and dark themes.\r\n  - The list is the text equivalent and the only interactive layer; the bubble group is `aria-hidden` with no \r\nfocusable children, and the card carries a one-sentence summary for screen readers.\r\n  - Copy: "Most misses in your recent LR practice", never "costing you points".\r\n  - List: continuous bars, questions per type, change "pts vs previous 30 days" only when both buckets have ≥8 \r\nquestions.\r\n  \r\n  ### 6. Ask with evidence\r\n  "Ask" on the main insight opens a draft in the existing inbox to Joshua, prefilled with the insight\'s evidence and a \r\ncomment field. Nothing sends without the student\'s Send. Label "Ask" (glossary). Uses the existing message insert path \r\nunchanged; no new message columns in this build.\r\n  \r\n  ### 7. Sure but wrong (gated)\r\n  Claude runs a read-only audit before PR 4: share of last-30-day `attempts` with non-null `confidence`, per write \r\npath, and whether each path captures confidence before feedback. Ship only if ≥60% coverage for at least half of \r\nactive students; else defer. Card: labelled 2x2 (1-2 unsure, 4-5 sure); neutral 3 and missing ratings are excluded and \r\ndisclosed in the coverage line ("[n] rated sure or unsure, [m] neutral, [k] unrated"); floor = 12 first attempts that \r\nland in a cell; tap a cell for its questions.\r\n  \r\n  ### 8. Quality bar (release requirement)\r\n  Cache-first paint on return visits; layout-matched skeletons on first load; no spinners. Count-up once per local \r\nday; motion transform/opacity only; none under `prefers-reduced-motion`. WCAG 2.2 AA incl. 200% text, focus order, \r\ncontrast on gradient and taupe. Accommodated timer used for any pace figure. Verification: a student test account at \r\n390 / 900 / 1440 and docked widths, dark class, keyboard only, reduced motion. Measure Home first paint before and \r\nafter PR 2.\r\n  \r\n  ### 9. Build order (PRs)\r\n  1. Layout + top panel + tiles + Book-a-session state on existing data, on top of #133 (closes #133).\r\n  2. RPCs (with auth tests) + adapters + insight engine + honest visuals + list + evidence drawer + `excludeAnswered`.\r\n  3. Registry (after Joshua\'s review) + diagnosis-to-action + Ask draft.\r\n  4. Sure but wrong, only if the audit passes.\r\n  Build two (separate plan): session brief; review then fresh check; Today shortcut; coach pinning with optional note; \r\nunified notifications (event identity, read semantics, reminders); RC lens; launcher Section tab reusing \r\n`rcSectionHref` (`rcSections.ts:131`) and `kind: \'section\'`.\r\n  \r\n  ## Key decisions & tradeoffs\r\n  - Diagnosis leads; coach notes never required.\r\n  - Bubbles for character, list for precision and accessibility.\r\n  - Frozen insight per visit.\r\n  - Small human-reviewed registry, not inference.\r\n  - Sure but wrong gated by audit, no new rating step.\r\n  - Unified notifications deferred whole, so its identity and read-state design is done once, properly.\r\n  \r\n  ## Risks / open questions\r\n  - RPC cost on first paint.\r\n  - Registry quality depends on one review.\r\n  - Student test account needed.\r\n  - Taupe/gradient contrast may force darker text than the mockup.\r\n  \r\n  ## Out of scope\r\n  Slim menu rail; notifications merge and bell popover; pinning; session brief; review loop; Today; RC lens; launcher \r\ntabs; goal score, streaks, PT score, month calendar; AI text; teacher (\r\n                        <span className="classroom-home-progress-note">\r\n                          About {hero.progress.estimateMinutes} minutes.\r\n                        </span>\r\n                      ) : null}\r\n                    </aside>\r\n                  ) : null}\r\n                </div>\r\n              </div>\r\n            </section>\r\n          ) : null}\r\n\r\n            {/*\r\n              A teacher has no assigned work by definition, so the card is dropped\r\n              rather than shown empty — an "assigned to you" panel on the admin\r\n              account only ever held verification leftovers.\r\n            */}\r\n            {model.isAdmin && !model.rows.length ? null : (\r\n            <section className="classroom-home-assigned" aria-label="Assigned to you">\r\n              <header className="classroom-home-card-head">\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-card-label">ASSIGNED TO YOU</span>\r\n                {model.newCount > 0 ? (\r\n                  <span className="classroom-home-badge">\r\n                    {model.newCount} NEW\r\n                  </span>\r\n                ) : null}\r\n              </header>\r\n              {model.rows.length ? (\r\n                <div className="classroom-home-rows">\r\n                  {model.rows.map((row) => (\r\n                    <WorkRow\r\n                      key={row.key}\r\n                      row={row}\r\n                      onOpen={() => handleOpenRow(row)}\r\n                      onMarkDone={onMarkDone}\r\n                      completing={completingKey === row.key}\r\n                    />\r\n\r\n                  ))}\r\n                </div>\r\n              ) : (\r\n                <p className="classroom-home-empty">\r\n                  {loading ? "Loading your work…" : "Nothing assigned right now."}\r\n                </p>\r\n              )}\r\n            </section>\r\n            )}\r\n          </section>\r\n          ) : null}\r\n        </div>\r\n        <div className="classroom-home-side">\r\n          <section className="classroom-home-card classroom-home-session classroom-home-next-session" aria-label="Next session">\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-session-icon" aria-hidden="true">\r\n              <CalendarClock size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} />\r\n            </span>\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-session-text">\r\n              <span className="classroom-home-card-label classroom-home-card-label-muted">\r\n                NEXT SESSION\r\n              </span>\r\n              <span className="classroom-home-session-value">{model.session ? <><strong>{new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit", hour12: true }).format(new Date(model.session.startsAt)).replace(/\\s?(AM|PM)$/i, "")}</strong><small>{new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { hour: "numeric", hour12: true }).format(new Date(model.session.startsAt)).match(/(AM|PM)$/i)?.[0]?.toLowerCase() ?? ""}</small></> : "Nothing booked"}</span>\r\n              {model.session ? <span className="classroom-home-session-date">{new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { weekday: "long", month: "long", day: "numeric" }).format(new Date(model.session.startsAt))} · {model.session.durationMin ? `${model.session.durationMin} minutes` : "Session"}</span> : <span className="classroom-home-session-date">Choose a time that works for you.</span>}\r\n              {model.session?.meta ? (\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-session-meta">{model.session.meta}</span>\r\n              ) : null}\r\n              {model.booking?.detail ? (\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-session-meta">{model.booking.detail}</span>\r\n              ) : null}\r\n            </span>\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-session-actions">\r\n              {/* No Reschedule pill: neither student-booking nor public-booking\r\n                  exposes a reschedule action. The booking pill itself follows\r\n                  the student\'s plan — credits go to the in-app picker, a\r\n                  pay-per-session student gets their own link. */}\r\n              <Pill\r\n                emphasis\r\n                onClick={() => {\r\n                  const target = model.booking?.href ?? "/booking";\r\n                  if (model.booking?.external) window.open(target, "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");\r\n                  else handleOpen(target);\r\n                }}\r\n              >\r\n                {model.booking?.label ?? (model.session ? "Book another" : "Book one")}\r\n              </Pill>\r\n            </span>\r\n          </section>\r\n        </div>\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      <div className="classroom-home-lower-row">\r\n        {model.access.messages ? <section className="classroom-home-thread classroom-home-tile" aria-label="From Joshua">\r\n          <header className="classroom-home-rule"><span className="classroom-home-card-label classroom-home-card-label-muted">{model.message ? `FROM ${model.message.from.toUpperCase()}` : model.isAdmin ? "MESSAGES" : "FROM JOSHUA"}</span><i aria-hidden="true" />{model.unreadMessages > 0 ? <span className="classroom-home-badge">{model.unreadMessages} NEW</span> : null}</header>\r\n          {model.message ? <div className="classroom-home-stack"><i className="classroom-home-note classroom-home-note-back" aria-hidden="true" /><i className="classroom-home-note classroom-home-note-front" aria-hidden="true" />{onClearMessage ? <SwipeableDeleteRow key={model.message.id} label={`message from ${model.message.from}`} rounded="rounded-xl" actionLabel="Clear" actionGerund="clearing" onDelete={() => onClearMessage(model.message!.id)}><MessagePreviewButton message={model.message} onOpen={handleOpen} /></SwipeableDeleteRow> : <MessagePreviewButton message={model.message} onOpen={handleOpen} />}</div> : <p className="classroom-home-empty">No messages yet.</p>}\r\n        </section> : null}\r\n        {model.access.activities ? <section className="classroom-home-card classroom-home-tile classroom-home-rrc-tile" aria-label="Read, Recall, Colour">\r\n          <span className="classroom-home-card-label classroom-home-card-label-muted">READ, RECALL, COLOUR</span><span className="classroom-home-session-value">Start a section</span><span className="classroom-home-session-meta">Read a full section aloud, then get it graded.</span><Pill emphasis onClick={() => handleOpen("/activities/sections")}>Choose a section</Pill>\r\n        </section> : null}\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {/* ── this week ────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}\r\n      <section className="classroom-home-week" aria-label="Next seven days">\r\n        <header className="classroom-home-rule">\r\n          <span className="classroom-home-card-label">NEXT SEVEN DAYS</span>\r\n          <i className="classroom-home-rule-draw" aria-hidden="true" />\r\n          <span className="classroom-home-week-range">{model.weekLabel}</span>\r\n        </header>\r\n        <div className="classroom-home-days">\r\n          {model.week.map((day) => {\r\n            const tone = day.isToday\r\n              ? " classroom-home-day-today"\r\n              : day.label\r\n                ? " classroom-home-day-item"\r\n                : day.isWeekend\r\n                  ? " classroom-home-day-rest"\r\n                  : " classroom-home-day-open";\r\n            const body = (\r\n              <>\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-day-head">\r\n                  <span className="classroom-home-day-abbr">{day.abbr}</span>\r\n                  <span className="classroom-home-day-num">{day.dayNum}</span>\r\n                </span>\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-day-label">\r\n                  {day.label ? (\r\n                    <>\r\n                      <i aria-hidden="true" />\r\n                      {day.label}\r\n                    </>\r\n                  ) : day.isWeekend ? (\r\n                    "Rest"\r\n                  ) : (\r\n                    "Open"\r\n                  )}\r\n                </span>\r\n              </>\r\n            );\r\n\r\n            // The band still reads without schedule access; it just stops being\r\n            // a control, rather than becoming a button onto a locked route.\r\n            return model.access.schedule ? (\r\n              <button\r\n                key={day.iso}\r\n                type="button"\r\n                className={`classroom-home-day${tone}`}\r\n                onClick={() => handleOpen("/schedule")}\r\n              >\r\n                {body}\r\n              </button>\r\n            ) : (\r\n              <div key={day.iso} className={`classroom-home-day${tone}`}>\r\n                {body}\r\n              </div>\r\n            );\r\n          })}\r\n        </div>\r\n      </section>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport default ClassroomHomeView;\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Warning: truncated output (original token count: 16028)\nTotal output lines: 1463\n\nWarning: truncated output (original token count: 18928)\nTotal output lines: 1784\n\n/**\r\n * The Classroom Home model.\r\n *\r\n * The page reads five things and states them once each: what to pick up, what\r\n * is assigned, what the coach said, when the next session is, and what the week\r\n * holds. Everything here is derived from work that actually exists in the\r\n * student\'s own record. Nothing on this page may be invented: a count, a due\r\n * date, or a progress bar that cannot be traced to a row is omitted, not\r\n * guessed. The view has honest empty variants for every block.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport { localDayISO, parseLocalDay } from "@/lib/localDate";\r\nimport type { SectionBriefing } from "@/lib/sectionBriefing";\r\n\r\nexport type HomeKind =\r\n  | "activity"\r\n  | "guided"\r\n  | "homework"\r\n  | "ta"\r\n  | "bootcamp"\r\n  | "plan"\r\n  | "recap"\r\n  | "note";\r\n\r\n/** Which of the three status dots a row carries. */\r\nexport type HomeTone = "activity" | "drill" | "recap";\r\n\r\nexport interface HomeRow {\r\n  key: string;\r\n  title: string;\r\n  kind: HomeKind;\r\n  tone: HomeTone;\r\n  /** "Activity · twelve prompts · due today" */\r\n  meta: string;\r\n  route: string;\r\n  /** "Open" for work, "Read" for a recap or note. */\r\n  actionLabel: string;\r\n  /** Orange pill (the one thing to do next) versus the muted ring. */\r\n  emphasis: boolean;\r\n  /** Never opened. Drives the "N NEW" badge; derived, never stored twice. */\r\n  isNew: boolean;\r\n  dueToday: boolean;\r\n  overdue: boolean;\r\n  /** Existing feed deadline, retained for the Continue action. */\r\n  dueAt?: string | null;\r\n  /** Work the student can close out by hand; reading matter cannot. */\r\n  canComplete?: boolean;\r\n  /**\r\n   * A timed section\'s instructions, carried on the row so Home can raise them\r\n   * over the stage rather than sending the student to a page to read them.\r\n   */\r\n  briefing?: SectionBriefing;\r\n\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface HomeProgress {\r\n  /** Present only when both halves are provable. */\r\n  completed: number | null;\r\n  total: number;\r\n  /** "prompts", "questions" — plural noun for the count. */\r\n  unit: string;\r\n  estimateMinutes: number | null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface HomeHero {\r\n  row: HomeRow;\r\n  eyebrow: string;\r\n  /** "ACTIVITY", "GUIDED DRILL" — the mono tag beside the meta line. */\r\n  kindLabel: string;\r\n  /** The row\'s meta with the kind dropped: the tag beside it already says it. */\r\n  meta: string;\r\n  prose: string | null;\r\n  primaryLabel: string;\r\n  progress: HomeProgress | null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface HomeMessage {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  from: string;\r\n  initial: string;\r\n  timeLabel: string;\r\n  body: string;\r\n  unread: boolean;\r\n  route: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface HomeSession {\r\n  /** ISO start. Null renders the honest "Nothing booked" variant. */\r\n  startsAt: string;\r\n  durationMin: number | null;\r\n  label: string;\r\n  meta: string | null;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** The plan-aware booking link shown next to the session card. */\r\nexport interface HomeBookingCta {\r\n  label: string;\r\n  href: string;\r\n  external: boolean;\r\n  detail: string | null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface HomeWeekDay {\r\n  iso: string;\r\n  abbr: string;\r\n  dayNum: number;\r\n  isToday: boolean;\r\n  isWeekend: boolean;\r\n  /** The single line under the date, or null for an empty day. */\r\n  label: string | null;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * What this particular person can actually reach.\r\n *\r\n * Home is a shortcut into surfaces the rail already gates, so it has to gate\r\n * itself the same way: a student without chat access must not be shown a\r\n * message card that dead-ends on a locked route, and one without schedule\r\n * access gets a week band that reads but does not click. Admins pass every\r\n * check, exactly as they do in the rail.\r\n */\r\nexport interface HomeAccess {\r\n  messages: boolean;\r\n  schedule: boolean;\r\n  activities: boolean;\r\n  /** Gates the Question finder. Without it there is nothing to find. */\r\n  drill: boolean;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface ClassroomHomeModel {\r\n  greeting: string;\r\n  displayName: string;\r\n  /** True when the viewer is staff looking at their own classroom. */\r\n  isAdmin: boolean;\r\n  access: HomeAccess;\r\n\r\n\r\n  dateline: string;\r\n  subtitle: string;\r\n  /** Calendar days until the student\'s test date; absent for null or past dates. */\r\n  daysToTest: number | null;\r\n  dueTodayCount: number;\r\n  hero: HomeHero | null;\r\n  rows: HomeRow[];\r\n  /** Chosen before the display list is capped at four rows. */\r\n  continueRow?: HomeRow | null;\r\n  newCount: number;\r\n  message: HomeMessage | null;\r\n  /** Every preview-worthy thread, newest first. `message` is its head. */\r\n  messages: HomeMessage[];\r\n  unreadMessages: number;\r\n  session: HomeSession | null;\r\n  /** The one booking link that matches this student\'s plan. */\r\n  booking: HomeBookingCta | null;\r\n  week: HomeWeekDay[];\r\n  weekLabel: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* ── small formatters ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\r\n\r\nconst ONES = [\r\n  "zero", "one", "two", "three", "four", "five",\r\n  "six", "seven", "eight", "nine", "ten", "eleven", "twelve",\r\n];\r\n\r\n/** Editorial voice: small numbers read as words in prose, digits everywhere else. */\r\nexport function spell(n: number): string {\r\n  return n >= 0 && n < ONES.length ? ONES[n] : String(n);\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function greetingFor(now: Date = new Date()): string {\r\n  const hour = now.getHours();\r\n  if (hour < 12) return "Good morning";\r\n  if (hour < 18) return "Good afternoon";\r\n  return "Good evening";\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** "MONDAY · AUGUST 3 · 2026" — three beats, with the date kept whole. */\r\nexport function datelineFor(now: Date = new Date()): string {\r\n  const part = (options: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions) =>\r\n    new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", options).format(now);\r\n  return [\r\n    part({ weekday: "long" }),\r\n    part({ month: "long", day: "numeric" }),\r\n    part({ year: "numeric" }),\r\n  ].join(" · ");\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The LOCAL calendar day a timestamp falls on.\r\n *\r\n * `iso.slice(0, 10)` is right for a bare `YYYY-MM-DD` and wrong for anything\r\n * carrying a time, because the stored timestamp is UTC. A section due at\r\n * 23:59 on the 30th in New York is stored as 03:59 on the 31st, and slicing\r\n * that reads the due date as tomorrow: the row loses its "due today" rank, the\r\n * hero picks the wrong assignment, and the label says "due Tuesday" for work\r\n * due Monday night. Every evening deadline in a western timezone hit this.\r\n */\r\nfunction calendarDay(iso: string): string {\r\n  if (/^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$/.test(iso.trim())) return iso.trim();\r\n  const parsed = new Date(iso);\r\n  return Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? iso.slice(0, 10) : localDayISO(parsed);\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction dayDiff(iso: string, today = localDayISO()): number | null {\r\n  const a = parseLocalDay(calendarDay(iso));\r\n  const b = parseLocalDay(today);\r\n  if (!a || !b) return null;\r\n  return Math.round((a.getTime() - b.getTime()) / 86_400_000);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** "due today" / "due tomorrow" / "overdue" / "due Friday" / "due 12 Sep". */\r\nexport function dueLabel(dueAt: string | null, now: Date = new Date()): string | null {\r\n  if (!dueAt) return null;\r\n  const diff = dayDiff(dueAt, localDayISO(now));\r\n  if (diff === null) return null;\r\n  if (diff < 0) return diff === -1 ? "one day overdue" : `${spell(-diff)} days overdue`;\r\n  if (diff === 0) return "due today";\r\n  if (diff === 1) return "due tomorrow";\r\n  if (diff <= 6) {\r\n    const date = parseLocalDay(calendarDay(dueAt));\r\n    return date\r\n      ? `due ${new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { weekday: "long" }).format(date)}`\r\n      : null;\r\n  }\r\n  const date = parseLocalDay(calendarDay(dueAt));\r\n  return date\r\n    ? `due ${new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { day: "numeric", month: "short" }).format(date)}`\r\n    : null;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** "assigned today" / "assigned yesterday" / "assigned 12 Sep". */\r\nexport function assignedLabel(createdAt: string, now: Date = new Date()): string | null {\r\n  const diff = dayDiff(createdAt, localDayISO(now));\r\n  if (diff === null) return null;\r\n  if (diff === 0) return "assigned today";\r\n  if (diff === -1) return "assigned yesterday";\r\n  if (diff < 0 && diff >= -6) return `assigned ${spell(-diff)} days ago`;\r\n  return `assigned ${new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {\r\n    day: "numeric",\r\n    month: "short",\r\n  }).format(new Date(createdAt))}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport const KIND_LABEL: Record<HomeKind, string> = {\r\n  activity: "Activity",\r\n  guided: "Guided drill",\r\n  homework: "Homework",\r\n  ta: "From Joshua",\r\n  bootcamp: "Bootcamp",\r\n  plan: "Weekly plan",\r\n  recap: "Recap",\r\n  note: "Note",\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport const KIND_TONE: Record<HomeKind, HomeTone> = {\r\n  activity: "activity",\r\n  guided: "drill",\r\n  homework: "drill",\r\n  ta: "activity",\r\n  bootcamp: "drill",\r\n  plan: "activity",\r\n  recap: "recap",\r\n  note: "recap",\r\n};\r\n\r\nconst PRIMARY_LABEL: Record<HomeKind, { start: string; resume: string }> = {\r\n  activity: { start: "Start the activity", resume: "Pick it back up" },\r\n  guided: { start: "Start the drill", resume: "Pick it back up" },\r\n  homework: { start: "Open the set", resume: "Pick it back up" },\r\n  ta: { start: "Open the assignment", resume: "Pick it back up" },\r\n  bootcamp: { start: "Open the bootcamp", resume: "Pick it back up" },\r\n  plan: { start: "Open the plan", resume: "Pick it back up" },\r\n  recap: { start: "Read it", resume: "Read it" },\r\n  note: { start: "Read it", resume: "Read it" },\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport function primaryLabelFor(kind: HomeKind, started: boolean): string {\r\n  const pair = PRIMARY_LABEL[kind];\r\n  return started ? pair.resume : pair.start;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The hero shows its kind as a tag, so the meta line beneath must not open by\r\n * saying it again. Falls back to the whole line when the kind is not the lead.\r\n */\r\nexport function stripKind(meta: string, kind: HomeKind): string {\r\n  const parts = meta.split(" · ");\r\n  if (parts.length > 1 && parts[0] === KIND_LABEL[kind]) return parts.slice(1).join(" · ");\r\n  return meta;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The one line under the greeting. Built from counts we can prove, and honest\r\n * when there is nothing: it never invents a streak or a projection.\r\n */\r\nexport function subtitleFor(dueToday: number, openWork: number, unread: number): string {\r\n  /** Every clause here opens a sentence, so the spelled number is capitalised. */\r\n  const Count = (n: number) => `${spell(n)[0].toUpperCase()}${spell(n).slice(1)}`;\r\n\r\n  const parts: string[] = [];\r\n  if (dueToday > 0) {\r\n    parts.push(\r\n      dueToday === 1\r\n        ? "One assignment is due today."\r\n        : `${Count(dueToday)} assignments are due today.`,\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n  const rest = Math.max(0, openWork - dueToday);\r\n  if (rest > 0) {\r\n    parts.push(\r\n      dueToday > 0\r\n        ? `${Count(rest)} more ${rest === 1 ? "is" : "are"} waiting.`\r\n        : `${Count(rest)} ${rest === 1 ? "assignment is" : "assignments are"} waiting for you.`,\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n  if (!parts.length) {\r\n    parts.push(\r\n      unread > 0\r\n        ? "Nothing is due today."\r\n        : "Nothing is due today. Drill whatever you feel like.",\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n  if (unread > 0) {\r\n    parts.push(unread === 1 ? "One unread message." : `${Count(unread)} unread messages.`);\r\n  }\r\n  return parts.join(" ");\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* ── the week band ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\r\n\r\nconst ABBR = ["SUN", "MON", "TUE", "WED", "THU", "FRI", "SAT"];\r\n\r\n/** The Monday-based week containing `now`, as seven local ISO dates. */\r\n/**\r\n * Seven days starting TODAY, not Monday to Sunday.\r\n *\r\n * A fixed calendar week hides tomorrow whenever today is Sunday, which is\r\n * exactly when a student most needs to see what is coming: work assigned for\r\n * Monday night simply was not on the strip. []) {\r\n      const iso = item.day_date.slice(0, 10);\r\n      if (span.has(iso)) entries.push({ iso, label: item.title });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    for (const item of itemsQuery.data ?? []) {\r\n      if (!item.due_at) continue;\r\n      const iso = localDayISO(new Date(item.due_at));\r\n      if (span.has(iso)) entries.push({ iso, label: item.title });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    for (const booking of bookingQuery.data?.bookings ?? Anything read or cleared is gone from both at the same instant -\r\n  // the two cannot disagree, because they read the same list.\r\n  const unread = access.messages ? messages.length : 0;\r\n\r\n  const model: ClassroomHomeModel = {\r\n    greeting: greetingFor(now),\r\n    displayName,\r\n    isAdmin: permissions.is_admin,\r\n    access,\r\n    dateline: datelineFor(now),\r\n    subtitle: subtitleFor(dueTodayCount, workRows.length, unread),\r\n    daysToTest: (() => {\r\n      const testDate = testDateQuery.data;\r\n      if (!testDate) return null;\r\n      const parsed = new Date(`${testDate.slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00`);\r\n      const delta = Math.round((parsed.getTime() - new Date(`${today}T00:00:00`).getTime()) / 86_400_000);\r\n      return Number.isFinite(delta) && delta >= 0 ? delta : null;\r\n    })(),\r\n    dueTodayCount,\r\n    hero,\r\n    rows: listRows,\r\n    continueRow: pickContinueWork(workRows, access, permissions.is_admin),\r\n    newCount: rows.filter((row) => row.isNew).length,\r\n    message,\r\n    messages,\r\n    unreadMessages: unread,\r\n    session,\r\n    booking,\r\n    week,\r\n    weekLabel: weekLabelFor(dates),\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  // Marking work done writes back to the assignment it came from, so the row\r\n  // leaves this page (and every other "what is due" count) while the classroom\r\n  // panel keeps it, labelled completed. Nothing is deleted.\r\n  const markDone = React.useCallback(\r\n    (key: string) => {\r\n      const row = assigned.find((candidate) => candidate.key === key);\r\n      if (!row || row.status === "completed") return;\r\n      setCompletingKey(key);\r\n      completion.mutate(\r\n        { sourceTable: row.sourceTable, refId: row.refId },\r\n        { onSettled: () => setCompletingKey(null) },\r\n      );\r\n/**\r\n * Which booking link a given student should be shown.\r\n *\r\n * A student on a package should never be sold anything on Home: they already\r\n * paid, so their link goes straight to the in-app time picker and says how many\r\n * sessions are left. One helper so Home, /booking and the schedule panel cannot disagree.\r\n */\r\n\r\nexport interface BookingCtaOffer {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  label: string;\r\n  provider: string;\r\n  url: string;\r\n  credits: number;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface BookingCtaPass {\r\n  status: string;\r\n  total_credits: number;\r\n  credits_remaining: number;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface StudentBookingCta {\r\n  /** Button copy. */\r\n  label: string;\r\n  /** Where the button goes. "").toLowerCase();\r\n  return status === "active" && pass.credits_remaining > 0;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function resolveStudentBookingCta({\r\n  pass,\r\n  offers = [],\r\n  hasUpcomingSession = false,\r\n}: {\r\n  pass: BookingCtaPass | null | undefined;\r\n  offers?: BookingCtaOffer[];\r\n  hasUpcomingSession?: boolean;\r\n}): StudentBookingCta {\r\n  // Credits in hand: pick a time, never buy.\r\n  if (activePass(pass)) {\r\n    const used = Math.max(0, (pass!.total_credits ?? 0) - pass!.credits_remaining);\r\n    const detail =\r\n      pass!.total_credits > 0\r\n        ? `${pass!.credits_remaining} of ${pass!.total_credits} sessions left`\r\n        : `${pass!.credits_remaining} sessions left`;\r\n    return {\r\n      label: hasUpcomingSession ? "Book another session" : "Book your next session",\r\n      href: IN_APP_BOOKING,\r\n      external: false,\r\n      detail: used >= 0 ? detail : null,\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Had a package, nothing left on it: offer the same one again when we can\r\n  // still identify it, so the student is not re-sold a different plan.\r\n  if (pass && (pass.total_credits ?? 0) > 1) {\r\n    const renewal =\r\n      offers.find((offer) => !isSingle(offer) && offer.credits === pass.total_credits) ??\r\n      offers.find((offer) => !isSingle(offer));\r\n    if (renewal) {\r\n      return {\r\n        label: `Renew ${renewal.label}`,\r\n        href: renewal.url,\r\n        external: !renewal.url.startsWith("/"),\r\n        detail: "Your package is used up",\r\n      };\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Pay per session.\r\n  const single = offers.find(isSingle);\r\n  if (single) {\r\n    return {\r\n      label: hasUpcomingSession ? "Book another session" : "Book a session",\r\n      href: single.url,\r\n      external: !single.url.startsWith("/"),\r\n      detail: null,\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return {\r\n    label: hasUpcomingSession ? "Book another" : "Book a session",\r\n    href: IN_APP_BOOKING,\r\n    external: false,\r\n    detail: null,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-action-title {\r\n  color: var(--home-ink);\r\n  font-family: var(--home-display);\r\n  font-size: 24px;\r\n  font-weight: 400;\r\n  line-height: 1.2;\r\n}\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-bento {\r\n  align-items: start;\r\n  gap: 22px;\r\n  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);\r\n  margin: 0;\r\n}\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-performance,\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-side { display: flex; The dark card flips the background to cream and only\r\n   overrode the colour, so the same 0.6 dropped it to 4.01:1 — under the 4.5:1\r\n   floor. Dark carries the de-emphasis in the colour instead, which keeps the\r\n   label quieter than the date without trading away legibility. */\r\nhtml.dark .classroom-root .classroom-home-day-today .classroom-home-day-abbr {\r\n  color: #5d564d;\r\n  opacity: 1;\r\n}\r\n\r\nhtml.dark .classroom-root .classroom-home-day-today .classroom-home-day-label {\r\n  color: var(--home-muted);\r\n  font-size: 12px;\r\n  line-height: 1.5;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* ── bento ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-bento {\r\n  align-items: start;\r\n  animation: classroomHomeRise 560ms var(--home-ease) 460ms both;\r\n  display: grid;\r\n  gap: 16px;\r\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1.24fr;\r\n  margin-top: 14px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-side {\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  flex-direction: column;\r\n  gap: 16px;\r\n  min-width: 0;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-card {\r\n  background: var(--home-white);\r\n  border-radius: 14px;\r\n  box-shadow: var(--home-card-shadow);\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  flex-direction: column;\r\n  min-width: 0;\r\n  overflow: hidden;\r\n    animation: none !important;\r\n    transition: none !important;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* ── responsive ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\r\n\r\n/* The bento's 1 : 1.24 split needs both columns to hold a card and a pill row.\r\n   Below this it stacks rather than compressing the session card's actions. */\r\n@media (max-width: 1080px) {\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-home-bento {\r\n    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n@media (max-width: 900px) {\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-home-hero-body {\r\n    align-items: stretch;\r\n    flex-direction: column;\r\n    gap: 24px;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-home-progress {\r\n    width: 100%;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-home-days {\r\n    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '---\r\nname: browser-harness\r\ndescription: "Always use browser-harness for any web interaction: automation, scraping, testing, or site/app work."\r\n---\r\n\r\n# browser-harness\r\n\r\nDirect browser control via CDP. For task-specific edits, use `agent-workspace/agent_helpers.py`. For setup, install, or connection problems, read https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness/blob/main/install.md.\r\n\r\nDomain skills are off by default. Set `BH_DOMAIN_SKILLS=1` to enable them; see the bottom section.\r\n\r\n**If `BH_DOMAIN_SKILLS=1` and the task is site-specific, read every file in the matching `$BH_AGENT_WORKSPACE/domain-skills/<site>/` directory before inventing an approach.**\r\n\r\n## Usage\r\n\r\n```bash\r\nbrowser-harness <<\'PY\'\r\nprint(page_info())\r\nPY\r\n```\r\n\r\n- Invoke as `browser-harness`. Use heredocs for multi-line commands.\r\n- Helpers are pre-imported. `run.py` calls `ensure_daemon()` before `exec`.\r\n- First navigation is `new_tab(url)`, not `goto_url(url)`.\r\n- The normal local flow attaches to the running Chrome/Chromium CDP endpoint. No browser ids or local profile selection.\r\n\r\n## Local Chrome\r\n\r\nIf the daemon cannot connect, run diagnostics:\r\n\r\n```bash\r\nbrowser-harness --doctor\r\n```\r\n\r\nIf Chrome remote debugging is not enabled, the harness opens:\r\n\r\n```text\r\nchrome://inspect/#remote-debugging\r\n```\r\n\r\nAsk the user to tick "Allow remote debugging for this browser instance" and click Allow if Chrome shows a permission popup. Then retry the same `browser-harness` command.\r\n\r\n## Remote Browsers\r\n\r\nUse Browser Use cloud for headless servers, parallel sub-agents, or isolated work. Authenticate once:\r\n\r\n```bash\r\nbrowser-harness auth login\r\n```\r\n\r\nOr import a key safely:\r\n\r\n```bash\r\nbrowser-harness auth login --api-key-stdin\r\n```\r\n\r\nPick a short made-up name; `r7k2` below is just a placeholder:\r\n\r\n```bash\r\nbrowser-harness <<\'PY\'\r\nstart_remote_daemon("r7k2")\r\nPY\r\n\r\nBU_NAME=r7k2 browser-harness <<\'PY\'\r\nnew_tab("https://example.com")\r\nprint(page_info())\r\nPY\r\n```\r\n\r\nWhen the task is done and a cloud browser is still running, ask directly: "Should I close this browser now?" If yes, run `stop_remote_daemon(name)`. Remote daemons bill until they stop or time out.\r\n\r\nDo not start a remote daemon and then keep using the default daemon. Use the same name for `BU_NAME`.\r\n\r\nCloud profile cookie sync reference: https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness/blob/main/interaction-skills/profile-sync.md.\r\n\r\n## Page Workflow\r\n\r\n- Screenshots first: use `capture_screenshot()` to understand visible state.\r\n- Clicking: screenshot -> read pixel -> `click_at_xy(x, y)` -> screenshot again.\r\n- After navigation, call `wait_for_load()`.\r\n- If the current tab is stale or internal, call `ensure_real_tab()`.\r\n- Use `js(...)` for DOM inspection or extraction when coordinates are the wrong tool.\r\n- Login walls: stop and ask. Exception: use available SSO automatically when Chrome is already signed in; still stop for passwords, MFA, consent, or ambiguous account choice.\r\n- Raw CDP is available with `cdp("Domain.method", ...)`.\r\n\r\n## Interaction Skills\r\n\r\nIf you get stuck on a browser mechanic, check https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness/tree/main/interaction-skills.\r\n\r\n- connection.md\r\n- cookies.md\r\n- cross-origin-iframes.md\r\n- dialogs.md\r\n- downloads.md\r\n- drag-and-drop.md\r\n- dropdowns.md\r\n- iframes.md\r\n- network-requests.md\r\n- print-as-pdf.md\r\n- profile-sync.md\r\n- screenshots.md\r\n- scrolling.md\r\n- shadow-dom.md\r\n- tabs.md\r\n- uploads.md\r\n- viewport.md\r\n\r\n## Design Constraints\r\n\r\n- Coordinate clicks default. CDP mouse events pass through iframes/shadow/cross-origin at the compositor level.\r\n- Keep the connection model simple: use the default daemon, `BU_NAME`, `BU_CDP_URL`, `BU_CDP_WS`, or `start_remote_daemon(...)`.\r\n- Core helpers stay short. Put task-specific helper additions in `$BH_AGENT_WORKSPACE/agent_helpers.py`.\r\n\r\n## Gotchas\r\n\r\n- `chrome://inspect/#remote-debugging` must be enabled for local Chrome control.\r\n- Chrome may show an "Allow remote debugging?" popup; wait for the user to click Allow.\r\n- Omnibox popups are not real work tabs.\r\n- CDP target order is not Chrome\'s visible tab-strip order.\r\n- `BU_CDP_URL` is an HTTP DevTools endpoint; the daemon resolves it to WebSocket.\r\n- Ask before leaving cloud browsers running; stop them with `stop_remote_daemon(name)` or `PATCH /browsers/{id} {"action":"stop"}`.\r\n\r\n## Domain Skills\r\n\r\nOnly applies when `BH_DOMAIN_SKILLS=1`. Otherwise ignore domain skills.\r\n\r\nWhen enabled, search `$BH_AGENT_WORKSPACE/domain-skills/<host>/` before inventing an approach. `goto_url(...)` returns up to 10 skill filenames for the navigated host.\r\n'}]
